## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The investor deck has a consistent identity: pale blue and navy palette, one light typeface, rounded white cards, and section dividers with a running header and page number. The content slides I read (12, 22, 23) are well organised, with clear column structure, strong figure hierarchy and a readable ranked-flow diagram on 23. The weaknesses are consequential: the deck opens with three pages of ASX announcement text (1-3) that read as a document, not a presentation, and several slides (22, 35, 44, 45) are dense and use very small type, with many footnotes and tables that cannot be read at a distance. Many divider pages (about 15 of 47) are empty, slide 24 is unlabelled, and some slides lack a headline statement of what to take away, so the reader has to find the point.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent card layout, the figure-plus-label hierarchy of slide 12, the stepped flow of slide 23 with its brace annotations, and the segment colour coding that carries through slides 22 and 24.

**Most useful repair:** Move the three-page ASX text to a separate document or cut it from the presentation, and break up slides 22, 35, 44 and 45 so the key numbers are legible rather than relying on tiny table and footnote text.

**What would change the score:** I would replace topic titles like 'Measuring our performance' and 'Capital breakdown by segment' with takeaway headlines, and cut or merge the empty divider slides.

_Confidence medium. 47 of 47 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and an acknowledgement of country, then moves into a chairman's introduction and a statement of why the strategy update is needed: slow overseas construction earnings, long-dated overseas development projects, and the aim to recycle capital. Its core sections follow in order: a delivery section on simplifying the business and cutting costs, a focused structure with segment roles, an Australia growth section with pipeline and capability data, a financials section with historical pro-forma tables, a capital management section covering the capital release unit, allocation framework and buy-back, and a segment summaries appendix with supporting data. It closes with a summary slide, the analyst Q&A marker, and a disclaimer. Slides worth studying include the strategy-at-a-glance metrics slide (slide 12), which pairs each segment with its key figures in one layout, the capital allocation hierarchy (slide 23), which shows a ranked flow of capital priorities as a diagram, and the Capital Release Unit breakdown (slide 22), which splits invested capital by segment and region. The deck uses headline-style titles and mostly keeps one idea per slide.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Lendlease strategy update deck from May 2024 have?**

The deck has 47 pages. Slide 5 contains the acknowledgement of country, and slide 17 opens the financials section.

**What does the Lendlease strategy update say about the Capital Release Unit?**

Slide 10 states that the Capital Release Unit has $2.8b of capital release anticipated by the end of FY25. Slide 22 also breaks down the invested capital in the Capital Release Unit by segment and region.

**What cost savings does the Lendlease strategy update set out?**

Slide 8 lists $125m of initial pre-tax run-rate cost savings within 12 months, along with a removal of the regional management structure. Slide 19 splits the further run-rate savings into $65m of employee costs and $60m of IT and other costs.

**What is the Lendlease capital allocation framework in the strategy update?**

Slide 23 says released capital will be reallocated based on a transparent hierarchy for capital deployment. Excess cash flow goes first to debt reduction, aiming for a gearing level of 5-15% by the end of FY26, followed by capital return and growth.

## Slides

### Slide 1

**Text**

ASX Announcement
lendlease
27 May 2024
Lendlease Group strategy update
Lendlease Group (“Lendlease” or “the Company”) today announces a strategy update that outlines
decisive actions to:
• Simplify its organisational structure and right size its cost base
• Focus on its market-leading Australian business and international Investments platform
• Recycle $4.5 billion of capital by completing transactions announced and underway,
exiting international construction and accelerating capital release from its offshore
development projects and assets
In combination, these steps leverage the Company’s competitive strengths and simplify the
business to enhance Lendlease’s integrated real estate business in Australia with international
investment management capabilities. Significant progress is expected within the next 12 to 18
months, with a range of important initiatives already well progressed.
The execution of the refocused strategy should enhance securityholder value through measures
that include:
• A structurally lower cost base with a further $125 million of annualised pre-tax savings,
to be achieved within 12 months and additional opportunities to follow
• Releasing approximately $3.42 per security of net tangible assets from a newly
established Capital Release Unit (CRU), with the majority anticipated by end of FY25
• A stronger balance sheet with significantly reduced gearing to within a revised target
range of 5%-15% anticipated by the end of FY26 (down from 10-20%)
• A phased return of capital to securityholders with an intention to undertake an initial
$500 million on-market buy-back
• A lower risk business positioned for profitable future growth with attractive Return on
Equity where Lendlease has a proven track record and competitive advantage
• Improved earnings on a like-for-like basis
Debt reduction and capital returns for securityholders will be prioritised through the implementation
of a disciplined Capital Allocation Framework that reflects the changed business priorities and
provides a transparent hierarchy for capital deployment.
Lendlease Chairman Michael Ullmer said, “We recognise that our security price performance and
securityholder returns have been poor as we have faced structural challenges and a prolonged
market downturn. We need to take significant action at an accelerated pace to deliver value for our
securityholders, capital partners and customers.

### Slide 2

**Text**

“Today we have announced the blueprint to position Lendlease for success – focussing on our
core strengths and competitive advantages.
“We have thought very carefully about the necessary strategic refocus and made some tough
decisions. I am confident that we have the right team and commitment to realise the value for our
securityholders that is inherent in our business. And I am determined to dedicate my final months
with this great company to ensure momentum builds toward the exciting future that lies ahead.”
Lendlease Group Chief Executive Officer and Managing Director, Tony Lombardo said, “Through
the decisive actions announced today, a new Lendlease is emerging. One that is firmly anchored in
the very best of our proud legacy, but less complex, more focused and fit for purpose. This new
Lendlease will be more easily understood by our people and customers, and transparent and
predictable for securityholders.
“By reshaping the portfolio, concentrating on our core competencies in markets where we have
proven we have the right to play, and the competitive advantage to win, the financial and
operational risk profile will be lower, and we believe the quality of our earnings ultimately higher
and more sustainable.
“Importantly, we do not launch this strategy from a standing start. Significant work has already
been undertaken and we anticipate making further positive announcements in the near-term on our
progress. We are well advanced on several transactions, and we have clear plans of action to
implement the necessary change to reorient the organisation. We are confident in the strategy and
have conviction in how we will execute.
“The optimum path we have chosen is built upon the creation of lasting economic value. We will
not walk away from commitments to our valued customers, and we will treat our people around the
world with the care and respect they deserve as our business changes.
“There is no question that the Australian business of Lendlease is market leading and unique in the
breadth and strength of its integrated capability and services. Moreover, the opportunities to grow
remain significant with a robust project pipeline that plays to our core competitive strengths,
especially in urban regeneration. We are exceptionally well placed to benefit from the key structural
shifts underway in the economy.
“We have retained our Investments platform in international markets for several compelling
reasons. Lendlease has deep relationships with major capital partners, and this presents an
appealing long-term growth vector through continuing to build our Funds Under Management. We
will leverage our scale and improve performance through active portfolio management, reducing
our co-ownership interests over time, and right sizing our cost base through removal of regional
cost structures that have weighed on performance.
“The establishment of the Capital Release Unit (CRU) is central to our new strategy. The CRU will
facilitate the recycling of $4.5 billion in capital of which $2.8 billion is anticipated by the end of
FY25. Our priority will be to pay down debt and efficiently return capital to securityholders. This is a
profound change. And is based upon making some very tough but necessary decisions.”
Lendlease Corporation Limited, ABN 32 000 226 228
Level 14, Tower Three, International Towers Sydney,
Exchange Place, 300 Barangaroo Ave, Barangaroo NSW
www.lendlease.com

- body at x 9.5, y 14.2, w 77.5, h 3 [ArialMT (Arial regular, sans-serif); font size 1.31% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: “Today we have announced the blueprint to position Lendlease for success – focussing on our / core strengths and competitive advantages. (Line spacing 1.5% of slide height (line pitch 1.5))
- body at x 9.5, y 18.7, w 79.7, h 5.8 [ArialMT (Arial regular, sans-serif); font size 1.31% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: “We have thought very carefully about the necessary strategic refocus and made some tough / decisions. I am confident that we have the right team and commitment to realise the value for our / securityholders that is inherent in our business. And I am determined to dedicate my final months / with this great company to ensure momentum builds toward the exciting future that lies ahead.” (Line spacing 1.5% of slide height; paragraph gap before next paragraph)
- body at x 9.5, y 26.4, w 81.5, h 6 [ArialMT (Arial regular, sans-serif); font size 1.31% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: Lendlease Group Chief Executive Officer and Managing Director, Tony Lombardo said, “Through / the decisive actions announced today, a new Lendlease is emerging. One that is firmly anchored in / the very best of our proud legacy, but less complex, more focused and fit for purpose. This new / Lendlease will be more easily understood by our people and customers, and transparent and / predictable for securityholders. (Line spacing 1.5% of slide height)
- body at x 9.5, y 35.5, w 77.9, h 5.8 [ArialMT (Arial regular, sans-serif); font size 1.31% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: “By reshaping the portfolio, concentrating on our core competencies in markets where we have / proven we have the right to play, and the competitive advantage to win, the financial and / operational risk profile will be lower, and we believe the quality of our earnings ultimately higher / and more sustainable. (Line spacing 1.5% of slide height)
- body at x 9.5, y 43.2, w 81.4, h 7.3 [ArialMT (Arial regular, sans-serif); font size 1.31% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: “Importantly, we do not launch this strategy from a standing start. Significant work has already / been undertaken and we anticipate making further positive announcements in the near-term on our / progress. We are well advanced on several transactions, and we have clear plans of action to / implement the necessary change to reorient the organisation. We are confident in the strategy and / have conviction in how we will execute. (Line spacing 1.5% of slide height)
- body at x 9.5, y 52.4, w 80.9, h 4.3 [ArialMT (Arial regular, sans-serif); font size 1.31% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: “The optimum path we have chosen is built upon the creation of lasting economic value. We will / not walk away from commitments to our valued customers, and we will treat our people around the / world with the care and respect they deserve as our business changes. (Line spacing 1.5% of slide height)
- body at x 9.5, y 58.5, w 81.5, h 7.4 [ArialMT (Arial regular, sans-serif); font size 1.31% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: “There is no question that the Australian business of Lendlease is market leading and unique in the / breadth and strength of its integrated capability and services. Moreover, the opportunities to grow / remain significant with a robust project pipeline that plays to our core competitive strengths, / especially in urban regeneration. We are exceptionally well placed to benefit from the key structural / shifts underway in the economy. (Line spacing 1.5% of slide height)
- body at x 9.5, y 67.7, w 79.7, h 8.9 [ArialMT (Arial regular, sans-serif); font size 1.31% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: “We have retained our Investments platform in international markets for several compelling / reasons. Lendlease has deep relationships with major capital partners, and this presents an / appealing long-term growth vector through continuing to build our Funds Under Management. We / will leverage our scale and improve performance through active portfolio management, reducing / our co-ownership interests over time, and right sizing our cost base through removal of regional / cost structures that have weighed on performance. (Line spacing 1.5% of slide height)
- body at x 9.5, y 78.4, w 80.8, h 5.8 [ArialMT (Arial regular, sans-serif); font size 1.31% of slide height; #000000; left aligned]: “The establishment of the Capital Release Unit (CRU) is central to our new strategy. The CRU will / facilitate the recycling of $4.5 billion in capital of which $2.8 billion is anticipated by the end of / FY25. Our priority will be to pay down debt and efficiently return capital to securityholders. This is a / profound change. And is based upon making some very tough but necessary decisions.” (Line spacing 1.5% of slide height)

### Slide 3

**Text**

The Strategy includes decisions which give rise to various impairments and charges. It is
anticipated these will be in the order of $1.150 - $1.475 billion pre-tax in FY24 and are not
anticipated to be recorded within core Operating Profit After Tax (OPAT). These impairments and
charges include a write down of goodwill attached to the US and UK construction businesses,
which arose as a consequence of the Bovis acquisition in 1999, impairments of certain overseas
development projects, where their intended use has changed, and other charges related to
redundancy and other break costs.

Lendlease maintains its FY24 guidance of 7 per cent return on Group equity, equating to
approximately $450m of OPAT. FY24 Group gearing is expected to be modestly above the mid-
point of the 10-20% target range. This guidance on both earnings and gearing is before the
anticipated provision for impairments and charges at FY24 and is subject to the completion of
transactions, including the announced sale of 12 Communities projects and the sale of our Asia
Life Sciences interests into a joint venture with Warburg Pincus, both of which remain subject to
conditions precedent.

ENDS

FOR FURTHER INFORMATION, PLEASE CONTACT:

Investors:
Michael Vercoe
Head of Investor Relations
Mob: +61 488 245 205

Media:
Stephen Ellaway
Executive General Manager, Corporate Affairs
Mob: +61 417 851 287

Authorised for lodgement by the Lendlease Group Disclosure Committee

Lendlease Corporation Limited, ABN 32 000 226 228
Level 14, Tower Three, International Towers Sydney,
Exchange Place, 300 Barangaroo Ave, Barangaroo NSW
www.lendlease.com

**Layout**

A single left-aligned text column starts at x 9.5% and runs to about 89% of the slide width, with no title, no header and no slide number. Body text starts at y 14.2% under a large empty top margin, and the contact details form a two-column block with the Investors column at x 9.5% and the Media column at x 39.8%. The corporate address footer sits at the bottom left at y 91.6%.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF. Plain white page with black Arial text throughout and no accent colours, rules, shapes, logos or images. Body text and headings are the same size (about 1.31% of slide height), with bold used for the ENDS heading, the Investors and Media labels, and the company name in the footer. The closing disclosure line is bold italic. The footer is set in smaller regular type (about 0.94% of slide height). Line spacing is about 1.5% of slide height between body lines.

### Slide 4

**Text**

Strategy
Update
May 2024
lendlease
Gurrowa Place, Melbourne (artist’s impression)

**Layout**

Two-panel full-bleed composition on a light blue ground: a large rounded photograph fills the left 78% of the slide, and a tall rounded navy panel sits on the right, overlapping the photo's right edge slightly at x 72.5%. Both panels sit with about 2.6% margin top and bottom. The title block sits in the upper part of the right panel, left-aligned at x 74.6%, with the date below it and the logo in the lower right of the panel. There is no footer, page number or slide number.

**Visuals**

Photograph: an artist's impression (rendering) of a dense mixed-use urban precinct seen from an elevated viewpoint. Several tall towers stand behind a public park: a pale glass tower with a green-roofed terrace on the left, a slim dark glass tower in the centre-left, a tall light-coloured residential tower with a warm brick-red lower podium, and a bronze-glass tower on the right. Low heritage-style terrace buildings with shaded awnings line the park edge. In the foreground, a grid of mature eucalyptus-style trees, lawns, and curved paths is populated by small figures. A sports court with a pink surface sits at the right edge. A faded, semi-transparent overlay of a building and a site hoarding appears in the lower left, ghosting the existing structures. Lighting is soft daylight under a pale blue sky with thin cirrus streaks; the lighting is warm on the bronze and brick facades. Dominant colours: sky blue #8DB7D9, glass grey #A9B3BA, warm bronze #B99B6B, brick red #A65A3A, park green #5E8B4A. The image is full-height within its rounded box, not cropped beyond its corners, and is boxed with rounded corners rather than full-bleed. Its lower-left corner carries a white caption. A white rounded-corner logo sits on the navy panel: a white folded-paper shape with the word lendlease in navy bold sans-serif.

**Design**

Background is a pale aqua-blue ground (#DEF6FC) showing as a thin border around two rounded panels. Title and date are white (#FFFFFF). The navy panel is #242444 with white text. The logo is white with navy #242444 lettering. Typeface: CircularXX Light, a geometric sans-serif in light weight, used in sentence case with no capitals treatment. Title size is about 10% of slide height per line with generous leading; date is about 3.3% of slide height; caption is about 1.5%. Panels have large corner radii of roughly 5% of slide height, no visible borders, and no shadows or gradients. The photo has no tint on its surface beyond its natural rendering.

**Layout**

Full-width light-blue slide with a large white rounded card spanning most of the slide below the title. The title sits top-left at the left margin (about 5.7% from the left). The card is split into a top row with three content blocks (map and text at left, a '>80%' stat with caption at centre, and text at right) separated by a vertical rule and a full-width horizontal rule, and a bottom row with three blocks (circle stat, org-chart icon with text, recycle icon with text) separated by vertical rules. Text is left-aligned in the outer blocks and centred in the middle blocks; footnote sits bottom-left and the footer right-aligned at bottom-right; the printed page number '4' is in an outlined circle at top-right.

**Visuals**

1) Dotted map of Australia: a photo-free pixel/dot-matrix illustration of the Australian continent, rendered as a grid of small square dots in pale gold (about #E3C88F) on the white card, placed at x 11.5%, y 28.4%, 17.1% by 27.8% (343 by 313 px); no outline, no crop, sits behind the '14.9%' figure and its caption, which overlap the dots. 2) Large numeral '>80%' with a short gold horizontal bar beneath it (about #D9A441, about 1.0% of slide height thick, centred, about 12.5% of slide width long) with caption below. 3) Thin vertical navy rule at x about 50% from y about 16% to 50%, and vertical navy rules at x about 37.4% and 62.6% spanning the bottom row (about y 62% to 89%); one full-width navy horizontal rule at y about 58.7% from x 8.9% to 91.1%, all about 0.1% thick. 4) Filled light-grey circle (#EBEBEB) with stat '0.6%' and caption inside it. 5) Org-chart icon in gold outline (#D9A441, stroke about 0.4% of height): one top rectangle connected by a vertical line and horizontal bus to three lower rectangles, centred above the 'Simplifying' text. 6) Recycling icon: three navy (#242444) chevron-arrow loops forming a triangle of arrows, stroke about 0.35% of height, on a rounded light-grey (#EBEBEB) square tile with corner radius about 2% of slide width, no shadow. 7) Small outlined navy circle around the page number '4' at top-right.

**Design**

Background light cyan #DEF6FC; white rounded card #FFFFFF with corner radius about 2.5% of slide width and no shadow or border. Title and all body text navy #242444; footnote and footer text grey #7F7F7F. Accents gold #D9A441 (underline, org icon) and pale gold #E3C88F (map dots). Typeface: Circular (CircularXX) Light, a geometric humanist sans-serif, regular weight throughout. Title about 7.4% of slide height; large stat numerals about 7.4% (14.9%) and 10% (>80%) and 6.3% (0.6%); body text about 3.0% of slide height; captions about 2.0% to 2.2%; footnote and footer about 1.3%; page number about 1.7%. Lines navy, hairline weight.

### Slide 8

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Lays out the simplification actions as a numbered grid with timing and scale for each, which keeps a complex plan easy to scan.

**Text**

Key actions to simplify Lendlease
5
1
Restructuring the organisation and reducing costs
2
Transactions announced and underway
3
Divesting international Construction
4
Releasing capital from international Development
0-12 months
0-12 months
0-18 months
staged
Removing regional management structure
$2.8b of assets on market 1
Targeting trade sales as going concerns
$1.7b of assets available for sale 1,2
$125m of initial pre-tax run-rate cost savings within 12 months
Communities (Australia) and Asia Life Sciences sales announced
Ongoing Construction business focused solely on Australia
No new Development origination in overseas markets
~250-350 FTE reduction (primarily international)
~760 FTE reduction (international) 3,4
~1,400 FTE reduction (international) 4
FTE reduced further based on lower activity
1. Net of anticipated impairments assumed for this presentation at the midpoint of the estimated range, for illustrative purposes only.
2. Net of funding for Engineering and Services and working capital associated with the international Construction business. 3. Excludes Communities (Australia). 4. Reduction via divestments.
Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024

**Layout**

Four equal columns of identical width, each stacking a navy numbered header box, a gold timing pill, and a white rounded card holding three centred text groups separated by short gold rules. The title sits top-left at the left margin, with a small outlined page number circle at top-right. Footnotes are bottom-left and the footer label is bottom-right, both on a shared bottom baseline; outer margins are about 5.6% of the width.

**Visuals**

None photographic. Four navy (#242444) rounded rectangles act as numbered header cards; each shows a gold numeral above a gold label. Below each sits a gold (#D7A04B) pill with a navy timing label. Below that, four white (#FFFFFF) rounded cards hold text and three short gold horizontal rules per card (about 4.6% of slide width, thin, dividing the three text groups). A small outlined circle with a navy outline and the numeral 5 sits at top-right. No charts, photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is a pale sky blue #DEF6FC. Title text and body text are navy #242444; header numerals and labels, pills' accent, and rules are gold #D7A04B; footnotes and footer are grey #7F7F7F. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to Circular (light weight for most text, bold for the short rule glyphs). Title is about 7.4% of slide height (large, light weight); header labels about 3.0%; numerals about 3.3%; body text about 2.2%; footnotes about 1.3%. Header boxes and cards have generous corner radii (about 15 to 20 px at this size), no visible shadows, no gradients, and flat fills with no borders.

**Layout**

Single-column, left-aligned title block sitting in the vertical middle of a full-bleed dark slide, inset by a thin rounded rectangular frame; the page number sits in the top-right corner inside the frame. No footer; no other elements.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid dark navy #242444. Solid dark navy background #242444 with a thin white (#FFFFFF) rounded-rectangle outline inset about 1.5% from each edge, corner radius about 6% of slide height, stroke about 1 px. Title in a thin/light geometric grotesque sans-serif (Circular-style, Thin weight), white #FFFFFF, two lines with tight leading, about 17.8% of slide height per line size, sentence case, no capitals except the initial D. Page number in Light weight sans-serif inside a thin white circle outline about 4.5% of slide height in diameter. No shadows, gradients or accent colours.

### Slide 10

**Text**

Delivering our strategy
7
Focused, simplified structure
Investments
Development
Construction
Capital Release Unit
International investment management capability
Australia only 1
Australia only
$2.8b of capital release anticipated by the end of FY25 2
Segment CEOs
Focused on segment performance
P&L accountability
CRU Senior Leadership
Focused on divesting assets and businesses to accelerate capital release
1. Capital Release Unit will continue to develop select projects with existing capital commitments in the short-to-mid-term prior to sale.
2. Net of anticipated impairments assumed for this presentation at the midpoint of the estimated range, for illustrative purposes only.
Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024

**Layout**

Four equal-width rounded cards sit in a row across the upper-middle of the slide, the first three dark and the last white, with a wider gap before the last card. The title sits top-left at about 10.6% from the top, left-aligned to a left margin of about 5.7%. A running header 'Delivering our strategy' with a circled page number 7 sits top-right, and the footnotes sit bottom-left with the footer line 'Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024' bottom-right, both on the same margins as the content.

**Visuals**

Four line icons in gold or navy, one above each card title. (1) Investments: a gold line-art chart icon, an L-shaped axis with a zigzag rising line ending in an arrowhead, about 14% to 17% across and 34% to 39% down. (2) Development: a gold line-art office building icon, a rectangular tower with a rounded top, a small antenna and a grid of window squares, about 36% to 39% across and 33% to 39% down. (3) Construction: a gold line-art hard hat icon, a dome with a brim and a centre seam, about 58% to 62% across and 34% to 39% down. (4) Capital Release Unit: a navy line-art recycling icon of three looping arrows forming a triangle, about 83% to 86% across and 33% to 39% down. Below the cards are two navy chevron (down-arrow) icons with horizontal rules: the left chevron at about 36% to 40% across and 64% to 67% down, with a long rule running from 6% to 35% and from 40% to 70% at about 65% down; the right chevron at about 83% to 86% across, 64% to 67% down, with short rules at about 75% to 82% and 87% to 94%. Short gold horizontal rules sit under each card title and under the two lower headings. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid pale aqua #DEF6FC. Flat design with a pale aqua background (#DEF6FC). Titles and the headline are dark navy #242444; card titles on the dark cards are gold #D7A04B; body text on dark cards is white #FFFFFF; body text on the white card and below the chevrons is navy #242444; accents are gold #D7A04B. Typeface is CircularXX, a geometric sans-serif: Light for the headline, card titles and body, Regular for the lower headings 'Segment CEOs' and 'CRU Senior Leadership', and Bold for the dash-rule text. Headline is about 7.4% of slide height; card titles and lower headings about 3.3%; body text about 2.2% to 2.6%; footnotes and footer about 1.3%. Cards are dark navy #242444 (three) and white #FFFFFF (one) with rounded corners of about 2% of slide width, no shadows, no borders. Gold rules are about 0.3% of slide height thick. Thin navy rules are about 0.2% thick. Chevrons are navy strokes about 0.4% thick.

### Slide 11

**Text**

Delivering our strategy
8
Strategic focus areas
Investments
Development
Construction
Capital Release Unit
Focus on performance, earnings and profitability
Expand development pipeline in Australia
Maintain efficiency and risk management
Unlock value for securityholders
Strengthen existing and add new capital partnerships
Increase capital partnering and focus on productivity and returns
Delivery capability for integrated model in Australia
Capital release from overseas developments and other businesses
Expand beyond traditional Develop-to-Core product offerings
Greater focus on capital partner preferences in product origination
Partner of choice for governments and other key clients
$2.8b of capital release anticipated by the end of FY25
Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024

**Layout**

Four vertical columns of equal card width sit on a light blue field, with the title top-left at about 10.6% from the top and the running header and page number top-right. Columns 1 to 3 are spaced evenly with gaps of about 2.4% of slide width, and the fourth column (Capital Release Unit) sits further right with a gap of about 4.4%. Each column has a header block above a tall white card holding three centred text groups separated by short gold rules; the footer sits bottom-right on the same right margin as the header, at about 94.3% of slide width, with a left margin of about 5.7%.

**Visuals**

Visual elements: eight short gold horizontal rules, two per column, one between each pair of text groups in the cards. Each rule is about 4.6% of slide width (about 90 px at 2000 px wide), about 2 px thick, colour #D7A04B, centred horizontally in its card, at about y 54% (upper rule) and about y 71% (lower rule). Also a single outlined circle containing the page number 8, at the top right. No photographs, charts, tables, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is a flat pale cyan #DEF6FC. Titles and body text are dark navy #242444; column headers in columns 1 to 3 are gold #D7A04B text on navy #242444 blocks; the Capital Release Unit header is navy #242444 text on white. Card fills are white #FFFFFF. Gold accent #D7A04B on rules. Footer grey #7F7F7F. Typeface is a geometric humanist sans-serif similar to Circular, light weight throughout, with no italics or capitals styling. Title is about 7.4% of slide height (large), column headers about 3.3%, body text about 2.6%, running header and footer about 1.3% to 1.7%. Shapes have rounded corners of about 20 px on a 2000 px slide (about 1% of slide width), flat fills, no borders, no shadows, no gradients. Page number is in a thin navy outlined circle about 2.1% wide.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Packs a segment-by-segment metric grid into one view, letting a reader compare each unit's key figures side by side.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent card layout, the figure-plus-label hierarchy of slide 12, the stepped flow of slide 23 with its brace annotations, and the segment colour coding that carries through slides 22 and 24.

**Text**

**Layout**

Four equal-width columns sit on a single row below the title, with the first three columns spaced evenly and a wider gap before the fourth column. The title Measuring our performance is top-left at about 10.6% from the top, left-aligned at 5.7% margin, with the section label Delivering our strategy and page number 9 in an outlined circle at top right. Three footnote lines sit left-aligned at the bottom beneath the cards, and the footer Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024 is right-aligned at the bottom right, with no logo.

**Visuals**

Four rounded-corner stat cards, each with a header tile and a white body, and each body holding four figures separated by short gold rules. Column 1 Investments: $3.2b invested capital, HY24; $50.4b FUM, HY24 (footnotes 1,2); $123m base mgmt. revenue, HY24 (2,3); 41.2% avg. EBITDA margin, FY19-23 (2,3,4). Column 2 Development: $1.8b invested capital, HY24; $10.4b work in progress, HY24 (5,6,7); $2.9b master planned or in conversion, HY24 (5,6); 18.2% avg. ROIC, FY19-23 (5,6). Column 3 Construction: $(0.6)b invested capital, HY24; $4.5b backlog revenue, HY24 (5,8); $3.4b avg. revenue, FY19-23 (5,8); 3.3% avg. EBITDA margin, FY19-23 (5,8). Column 4 Capital Release Unit: $3.42 NTA per security, HY24 (9,10,11); $2.8b assets on market, FY24-25 (11); $1.7b assets available for sale, FY25-28+ (11,12); closing statement Anticipated positive earnings contribution to the Group. Gold rules #D7A04B, about 4.5% of slide width, sit under each figure in every card at about 52.1%, 64.7% and 77.3% of slide height. No charts, photographs or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid pale cyan #DEF6FC. Pale cyan background #DEF6FC with white cards and dark navy #242444 header tiles. Titles and body text are in a geometric sans-serif light weight (CircularXX-Light style), with the closing statement in Regular. The title is about 7.4% of slide height, big numerals about 5.2%, card labels about 1.7% and footnotes about 1.3%. Gold accent #D7A04B is used for column headers and rules; footnotes and footer are grey #7F7F7F. Header and card corners are rounded at about 1.5% of slide width, with no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 13

**Text**

Australia is our key growth focus
10

**Layout**

Single full-slide composition on a dark navy field with a thin rounded-rectangle outline inset about 1.5% from the edges; the headline is left-aligned, set in two lines in the vertical middle of the slide starting near 5.7% from the left edge, with the page number in a small circle at the top right corner.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background solid navy #242444. Headline and page number in white #FFFFFF, a thin geometric sans-serif (CircularXX Thin) at about 17.8% of slide height for the headline, set in regular sentence case with tight but open tracking. **Layout**

Full-bleed 6-column by 5-row grid of photographs covering the whole slide edge to edge with no margins, each tile about 16.7 percent wide and 20 percent tall. A large white rounded card sits centred over the middle of the grid, spanning columns 3 and 4 and rows 2 to 3, and carries the title in three centred lines. Each photo has its caption at the bottom-left, inset about 0.6 percent from the left edge. The footer 'Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024' sits bottom-right in grey, and a small page number '11' sits top-right, in the upper-right area above the card.

**Visuals**

Photographic mosaic of 30 tiles in a 6 by 5 grid, all full-bleed and unframed, with no gaps between tiles. Tiles from left to right, top to bottom: row 1: Caltex House, Sydney (black and white aerial view of a rooftop sign reading CALTEX on a stepped building); Australia Square, Sydney (black and white view of a tall circular tower among high-rise blocks); Sydney Opera House (warm sepia-toned view of the sails across the harbour water); MLC Centre, Sydney (tall pale tower against a blue sky, viewed from below); The Regent of Sydney (gold-toned hotel facade with angled balconies, low sun); Tullamarine Airport, Melbourne (aerial view of a star-shaped terminal roof with radiating structures). Row 2: Sydney Olympic Park (aerial of a stadium with a white arched roof and surrounding sports grounds); Aurora Place, Sydney (blue-sky view of tall glass towers, partly hidden by the card); Sydney Airport (interior of a terminal with red seats and a red gate sign reading 44 and a flight display, partly hidden by the card); National Museum of Australia, Canberra (interior of a curved timber and steel canopy, partly hidden by the card). Row 3: Deutsche Bank Place, Sydney (dark blue glass tower with a rooftop sign reading Deutsche Bank, viewed from below); Darling Quarter, Sydney (public plaza with palm trees, a water play area and a glass-roofed building at dusk, partly hidden by the card); Victoria Harbour, Melbourne (marina with white yachts in the foreground and a modern dark-clad building at right, under a pale sky, partly hidden by the card); Gold Coast University Hospital (aerial of a campus of pale buildings with a green roof and road). Row 4: Adelaide Oval (exterior of a curved stadium with a green and blue roof at blue hour); Royal Children’s Hospital, Melbourne (atrium with a large colourful sculpture and people seated); Rod Laver Arena, Melbourne (aerial of a white stadium with a curved roof and a city skyline behind at dusk); Gold Coast Commonwealth Games (purple and magenta graphic with the text Gold Coast 2018 and SHARE THE DREAM over a rows-of-seats background); Darling Square, Sydney (circular bronze-toned building with a crowd at dusk); Western Sydney Stadium (interior of a stadium with a crowd and a green pitch under a roof). Row 5: Air Traffic Control (control tower against a blue sky with clouds, viewed from below); Melbourne Quarter (public square with trees, a glass canopy and people); Sunshine Coast University Hospital (interior atrium with timber ceiling and glass walls); Barangaroo South, Sydney (cluster of tall towers, one with a curved glass facade, and a harbour ferry in the foreground); Data Centres (interior of a grey-floored hall with rows of black server cabinets under lights); Salesforce Tower, Sydney (view up at a tall glass tower among other office towers at dusk). Lighting is mostly daylight, with warm sunlight on the Sydney and Melbourne buildings. Dominant colours: blue sky, grey-silver glass, warm sepia and gold, and a magenta-purple graphic tile. Each tile has a dark gradient scrim at its bottom edge under the caption, and the photographs are tinted only by the scrim.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed photographic grid over a light cyan #DEF6FC fill, with a pure white rounded card in the centre. The title is dark navy #242444 in CircularXX Light, a geometric sans-serif, set at about 8.9 percent of slide height, centred. Captions are white CircularXX Regular, a geometric sans-serif, at about 1.3 percent of slide height, left aligned. The footer is grey #7F7F7F CircularXX Light at about 1.3 percent. The white card has rounded corners of roughly 2.7 percent of slide height and no border or shadow. Each caption sits on a dark translucent gradient scrim over the bottom of its tile, and no other shadows or gradients are visible.

**Layout**

Four-part slide on a light blue field: a title top-left at about 5.7% from the left and 10.6% from the top, with a header at top-right reading 'Australia' and a circled page number '12'. A large white rounded card spans the middle (x 5.7 to 94.3%, y 23.7 to 64.6%); inside it, a donut diagram sits on the left and a headline with body text is left-aligned at x 32.8% beside it. Below the card, four equal columns (about 24% wide each) are separated by thin vertical rules, each with a gold icon tile above a centred two- or three-line caption. A small grey footer sits bottom-right at y 96.1%. Margins are about 5.7% left and right.

**Visuals**

Donut diagram of three segments forming a ring around a white centre, with thin curved outline arcs on the left and right and curved labels along the outer edge. Segments: Investments in gold #D7A04A (left, about 90 degrees from top to lower-left), Development in navy #242343 (right, from top to lower-right), Construction in grey #999999 (bottom, between them, slightly narrower). Labels: 'Integrated Business Model' curved along the top-left outside edge, 'Proven Track Record' curved along the bottom outside edge, 'Investments' and 'Development' and 'Construction' set inside their segments and rotated to follow the ring. The chart has no numeric values, axes or legend. Four icon tiles, each a gold #D7A04A rounded square with a white thin-line icon: (1) a multi-storey office building with window grid; (2) two hands shaking; (3) a line chart with an upward trend arrow inside an L-shaped axis; (4) a seedling sprout with two leaves. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #DEF6FC. Light sky-blue background #DEF6FC with a pure white rounded card (corner radius about 1.5% of slide width, no shadow, no border). Title and headline in dark navy #242444, body text in the same navy, the footer in mid grey #7F7F7F. Accents are gold #D7A04A (icon tiles, Investments segment) and navy #242343 (Development segment). Typeface is CircularXX-Light, a geometric sans-serif in light weight. Title size about 7.4% of slide height, headline about 4.1%, body and captions about 3.0%, header and footer about 1.3% to 1.7%. Thin dividers in navy at about 1 px. Icons are white strokes on gold tiles with rounded corners of about 8% of tile width.

Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024

**Layout**

A full-bleed light blue slide with a large white rounded card spanning most of the width and height below the header, split into a left table panel and a right capabilities panel by a thin vertical divider. The title and subtitle are left-aligned at the top-left margin (about 5.7% from the left edge). The section label Australia and the circled page number 13 sit at the top right, and the two footnote and source lines sit at the bottom left and bottom right, outside the card.

**Visuals**

One table, no photographs, logos or charts. Table columns: unlabelled row-label column; End value (with footnote 1); No. of projects by stage, spanning two sub-columns Advanced (footnote 2) and Early stage (footnote 3). Rows: Urban regeneration (mixed-use) with End value $25b, Advanced 3, Early stage 11; Residential with End value $8b, Advanced -, Early stage 7; Commercial with End value $7b, Advanced 3, Early stage 1; total row with $40b (End value), $13b (Advanced), $27b (Early stage). The End value cells for the three category rows are light blue (#DEF6FC) boxes; the End value and No. of projects header cells are grey (#EBEBEB) rounded boxes; the total row cells are solid navy (#242444) boxes with white text. A dark navy horizontal rule sits under the column headers and under the total row, with thin grey rules between category rows. A vertical navy divider line separates the table panel from the right text panel. The circled page number is a thin navy outline ring around 13.

**Design**

Background light cyan #DEF6FC with a white card #FFFFFF. Titles and body text navy #242444; footnotes and footer mid grey #7F7F7F; accent light blue #DEF6FC cells, grey #EBEBEB header cells, navy #242444 total cells. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif (CircularXX family) in Light for the title, subtitle, table and body text and Regular for the right-panel sub-headings. Title is about 7.4% of slide height, subtitle about 4.1%, body and table text about 2.6% to 3.0%, bullets and sub-headings about 2.2%, footnotes about 1.3%. Lines are thin navy strokes of about 0.1% to 0.2% of slide height. The card has rounded corners of roughly 1.5% of slide width. No shadows or gradients.

Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 5.7% from the left edge and 10.6% from the top, with a small 'Financials' section label and a circled page number '15' at top right. Below the title, one large white rounded card spans about 5.7% to 94.3% of the width and 23.8% to 93.6% of the height; inside it, three donut columns (I / D / C, Capital Release Unit, Group) with headings and underlines sit over a table on the left, a vertical divider at about 72% of the width separates a Group target donut and grey ROE callout on the right. Footnotes sit bottom left at about 95% of the height and the source line sits bottom right, both left aligned or right aligned respectively on a common baseline band.

**Visuals**

Four donut charts and one table and one legend. Chart 1, I / D / C: donut, centre label $589m avg. EBITDA, FY19-23 (segment EBITDA, superscript 2,5); segments Investments 35% (#242444, upper right), Development 46% (#DEF6FC, lower left and bottom), Construction 19% (#D7A04B, upper left); no axes, no units beyond percent and $m. Chart 2, Capital Release Unit: donut centre label $58m avg. EBITDA, FY19-23 2,5; a single full ring in CRU grey #EBEBEB labelled 100% in the file but the label is #EBEBEB on #EBEBEB and not legible on the slide. Chart 3, Group: donut centre label $647m avg. EBITDA, FY19-23 2,5; segments Investments 32% (#242444, upper right), Development 42% (#DEF6FC, bottom), Construction 17% (#D7A04B, left), CRU 9% (#EBEBEB, top, small wedge). Chart 4, Group target (post-simplification) 1: donut with centre label EBITDA 2,5; segments Investments 50% (#242444, top half), Development 35% (#DEF6FC, bottom right), Construction 15% (#D7A04B, lower left); no separate legend, same colour key as legend. Legend (left of chart 1 at about x 8.3%, y 52.7 to 63.1%): four square swatches, each about 0.9% wide and 1.6% high, with labels Investments (#242444), Development (#DEF6FC), Construction (#D7A04B), CRU (#EBEBEB). Table: heading '$m, FY19-23 avg:' above three columns (I / D / C, Capital Release Unit, Group; the column headers are the chart headings above) and three rows: Operating PAT 3,5,6 with 312, (156), 156; Operating ROE 4,5,6 with 11.8%, (3.7%), 2.3%; Group ROE 4,5,7 with 10.0%, (6.1%), 0.2%. Negative figures are in parentheses. Highlighted: the Group ROE row is the bottom row with a heavy rule above it. Callout: grey rounded box reading Group ROE 4,5,7 sustainably above cost of equity.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #DEF6FC full-bleed behind the whole slide. Light sky-blue slide background #DEF6FC with a white rounded card (#FFFFFF) holding all content, corner radius about 3% of slide width, no shadow, no border. Titles and key figures in navy #242444; body and table text #242444; accent colours for chart segments are navy #242444, pale blue #DEF6FC, gold #D7A04B and light grey #EBEBEB; footnotes and source line in grey #7F7F7F. Typeface is CircularXX, a geometric grotesque sans-serif: Light weight for the title, headings, body and table, Regular weight for the centre EBITDA labels and the superscript line 2,5. Title is about 7.4% of slide height; headings about 3.0%; body and table text about 2.2%; chart labels about 2.0%; footnotes about 1.3%. Thin navy rules (about 0.2% of height) under headings and table rows; the table header and bottom rules are navy, the middle rules are grey. Vertical divider is a thin navy line. Donuts are flat with white gaps between segments, no gradient, no shadow.

**Layout**

Single-slide grid: a large white rounded panel fills the body of the slide from about 5.7% to 94.3% horizontally and 23.8% to 93.6% vertically, with the title at top left above the panel. The panel is split into a left text column (about 8% to 25% width) and a right area holding the bar chart (about 28% to 61%) with a connector chevron and a Note 7 table (about 65% to 92%). The section label 'Financials' and page number 16 sit top right; footnotes sit bottom left and the document name bottom right, both aligned to the 5.7% and 94% margins.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled 'Employee overhead expense, $m' with superscript footnote marker 1, with a thin dark rule beneath the title. Vertical axis not shown; no axis tick labels. Horizontal category axis with categories FY21, FY22, FY23. One series, labelled 'Employee costs net of project and asset recoveries', shown as solid dark navy bars with values printed above each bar: FY21 = 592, FY22 = 508, FY23 = 384 (no estimates; values are printed). Bars are drawn to scale against a heavy navy baseline; bar heights are proportional to the values. No highlighted points. Legend below the chart: a small navy square followed by the series name. A right-pointing chevron sits between the chart and table, connected by two thin vertical navy lines that span the chart height. Table 'Note 7. Other Expenses' with superscript 2 as header, columns labelled (blank) and FY23. Rows: Total Employee Benefit Expense 1,878; Less: Recoveries through projects (1,570); Superannuation accumulation plan expense 85; Net defined benefit plans expense (9); Total 384 (Total row in regular weight). Header is underlined with a heavy navy rule; rows separated by thin grey rules.

**Design**

Background: Solid pale aqua #DEF6FC. Pale aqua background #DEF6FC with a white rounded panel #FFFFFF. All text and chart elements are dark navy #242444. Footnotes and document name are mid grey #7F7F7F. Typeface is a geometric humanist sans-serif in CircularXX Light (Regular for the table header and total). Title is large light weight at about 7.4% of slide height; body and table text about 1.85% to 2.2% of slide height; footnotes about 1.3%. Thin navy rules (about 2 px) under the chart title, the table header and the chart baseline; grey hairlines between table rows. Panel has a generous corner radius (about 2.5% of slide width) and no shadow. Flat fills, no gradients.

### Slide 20

**Text**

Financial impacts to accelerate our strategy / Financials / 17 / Impairments and charges are required / to accelerate the release of capital / Estimated range (before tax) / Overseas development project impairments / $450m / $550m / Redundancy, tenancy and other break costs / $75m / $150m / Construction goodwill, deferred tax assets, / and other costs 1 / $625m / $775m / Impairments of overseas developments relate to projects with substantial office and/or residential condominium exposure where there has been a material change in development strategy / The decision to divest international construction operations will result in the impairment of related goodwill and deferred tax assets / These decisions will also result in redundancy, tenancy and other break costs / Anticipated impairments and charges will be assessed at 30 June 2024 and are expected to: / – increase FY24 gearing by 0.6-0.7% 2 / – decrease net tangible assets per security by 80-118 cents 2 / 1. Includes a portion of goodwill and deferred tax assets related to overseas development projects. 2 Based on anticipated average securityholders’ equity at the end of FY24. / Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024

**Layout**

Single-slide content layout with a full-bleed light blue background and a large white rounded card inset about 5.7% from the left and right edges and from 23.8% to 93.6% of the height. The title sits top-left at about 10.6% from the top, left-aligned to the 5.7% margin. Inside the card, a two-column arrangement: a left table column (about 8% to 61%) with a heading above it, and a right column of bullets (about 63% to 92%) separated by a vertical dark rule at about 61.3% of the width. A small section label Financials and a circled page number 17 sit top-right; footnotes sit bottom-left and a document label bottom-right, both at about 96% of the height.

**Visuals**

A table with a heading row and three data rows, plus a vertical divider rule and a set of circled/outlined marks. Table: columns are an unlabelled row-label column (left, about 8.3% to 38% of slide width), then a column headed Estimated range (before tax) with two value columns. The header cell over the value columns is a light grey #EBEBEB fill spanning about 38.6% to 58.7% width, 47.2% to 55.6% height. Rows: (1) Overseas development project impairments: $450m and $550m; (2) Redundancy, tenancy and other break costs: $75m and $150m; (3) Construction goodwill, deferred tax assets, and other costs (footnote 1): $625m and $775m. Each value sits inside a rounded-corner light blue #DEF6FC cell about 9.2% wide and 8.4% high, with no border, on the white card. Horizontal rules: a dark #242444 line about 0.3% thick at about 44.6% height spanning the table width (about 8.3% to 61.6%); thin grey #7F7F7F-style hairlines, about 0.1% thick, under the header row and under each data row. A vertical dark #242444 divider line about 0.2% thick at about 61.3% x from about 28.8% to 88.9% of height. No photographs, logos or icons. Slide number 17 is shown inside a thin dark outlined circle (about 92.2% to 94.3% x, 5.0% to 8.8% y).

**Design**

Background light cyan #DEF6FC. Card white #FFFFFF with rounded corners of roughly 3% of slide height and no shadow. Title dark navy #242444, set in a light geometric sans-serif (CircularXX-Light, a Circular-like grotesque), regular weight, about 7.4% of slide height (approx. 5.6% for lowercase x-height cap rendering). Body text #242444 in the same light sans at about 2.2% to 2.4% of slide height. Heading in card at about 3.0% of height. Footnotes and footer in #7F7F7F grey at about 1.3% of height. Accents: dark navy #242444 rules and circle outline; value cells light cyan #DEF6FC; header cell #EBEBEB. Bullets are small dark dots in Arial-style #242444. Dashes used for sub-bullets. Line shapes are thin and square-ended; corners of cells are slightly rounded. No gradients.

### Slide 21

**Text**

Capital management

**Layout**

A single left-aligned title set in two lines ("Capital" / "management") sits in the left-centre of the slide, starting about 5.7% from the left edge and vertically centred around the middle of the slide. A thin white rounded-rectangle frame inset about 1.5% from each edge encloses the whole slide, and a small page number sits top right inside the frame. There is no footer, subtitle or body text, and the right half of the slide is empty.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid colour #242444 (dark indigo). Solid dark indigo background #242444 with a single thin white rounded-rectangle outline. Title in a thin, light geometric sans-serif (CircularXX Thin style), white #FFFFFF, lowercase except the initial capital, set very large at about 17.8% of slide height with tight leading and default letter-spacing. Page number in CircularXX Light, white #FFFFFF, about 1.7% of slide height, inside a thin white outlined circle. Frame stroke is white #FFFFFF about 2 px wide with a corner radius of about 75 px (roughly 6.7% of slide height). No shadows, gradients or other ornaments.

### Slide 22

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a stacked capital breakdown with clear totals and adjustments to show how invested capital moves through the impairment assumptions.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent card layout, the figure-plus-label hierarchy of slide 12, the stepped flow of slide 23 with its brace annotations, and the segment colour coding that carries through slides 22 and 24.
- Suggested repair: Move the three-page ASX text to a separate document or cut it from the presentation, and break up slides 22, 35, 44 and 45 so the key numbers are legible rather than relying on tiny table and footnote text.

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 5.7% from the left edge, with a section header 'Capital management' and page number 19 in a thin outlined circle at top right. A single large white rounded panel spans about 5.7% to 94.3% horizontally and 23.8% to 93.6% vertically holding three columns (Invested capital, I / D / C, Capital Release Unit) each with a heading, a rule and stacked rows, plus a side column of asset totals at right; chevrons and vertical dividers separate the columns. Totals sit under each column, and footnotes run in two lines at the bottom left with the source line at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Diagram/flow of stacked tiles in three columns with chevron arrows: Column 1 'Invested capital' stacks Investments $4.0b (navy tile), Development $7.2b (light blue tile), Construction ($0.6b) (gold tile), Non-Core ($0.4b) (grey tile), Impairments ($1.3b) 7 (white outlined tile), totalling Total: $8.9b 7. A chevron points from column 1 to column 2. Column 2 'I / D / C' stacks $3.2b (navy tile) with grey detail box listing $1.3b Australia 2, $0.6b Europe, $1.0b Asia, $0.3b Americas 3; $1.8b (light blue tile) with grey box Development (Australia) 4; ($0.6b) (gold tile) with grey box Construction (Australia) with bullets ($1.2b) working capital and $0.6b goodwill; totalling Total: $4.4b. A chevron points from column 2 to column 3. Column 3 'Capital Release Unit' stacks $0.8b (navy tile) with grey box $0.4b US Military Housing and $0.4b Retirement Living (Aus) 5; $5.4b (light blue tile) with grey box $4.1b Development (int'l) 6 and $1.3b Communities (Aus); ($0.0b) (gold tile) with grey box Construction (international) with bullets ($0.5b) working capital and $0.5b goodwill; ($0.4b) (grey tile) with grey box Engineering and Services (Non-Core); ($1.3b) 7 (white outlined tile) with white outlined box ($0.8b) tangible assets and ($0.5b) goodwill; totalling Total: $4.5b 7. A right-hand side column shows Assets on market $2.8b 7 with rows $1.3b Communities (Aus), $0.4b US Military Housing, $0.4b Retirement Living (Aus) 5, $0.7b Development (int'l), and Assets available for sale $1.7b 7 with rows ($0.5b) Construction (int'l) 7, $2.5b Development (int'l) 6,7, and ($0.4b) Engineering and Services (in run-off). No photographs, logos or icons; all visuals are flat tiles, rules, dividers and chevrons. Nothing is cropped or overlaid.

**Design**

Background: Solid light sky blue #DEF6FC, with a white rounded content panel on top. Flat, minimal corporate infographic. Slide background light sky blue #DEF6FC; main content panel white #FFFFFF with large rounded corners (about 3% radius) and no shadow. Titles and body text navy #242444. Accent gold #D7A04B for the Construction tiles; light blue #DEF6FC tiles for Development; navy #242444 for Investments and top-value tiles; neutral grey #EBEBEB for Non-Core and detail boxes; white tiles with navy 1px outline for Impairments. Typeface is a geometric humanist sans in a Light weight (recognisable as Circular, a sans-serif); bold-like emphasis only on the 'Construction (Australia)' and 'Construction (international)' labels, which are Regular weight. Title is about 7.4% of slide height; column headings about 2.6%; tile labels about 2.6%; detail text and header about 1.7%; asset heading about 2.2%; footnotes and source about 1.3%. Rules are thin navy lines about 0.2% of slide height; dividers are vertical navy lines about 0.1% width. Chevrons are open navy strokes with rounded joins.

### Slide 23

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Turns the capital allocation priorities into a ranked diagram that makes the order of spending clear at a glance.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent card layout, the figure-plus-label hierarchy of slide 12, the stepped flow of slide 23 with its brace annotations, and the segment colour coding that carries through slides 22 and 24.

**Text**

Capital allocation framework
Capital management
20
Released capital will be
reallocated based on our
transparent hierarchy for
capital deployment:
Operating productivity
Capital productivity
Net operating cash flow
Maximise cash flow through
lower costs, productivity,
technology and culture
Strong balance sheet
30-50% distribution payout ratio 1
Distribution linked to
business performance
Excess cash flow
Debt reduction
To target gearing level (5-15%) by the end of FY26 2
Capital return
Security buy-backs, additional distributions
Growth
Subject to strict investment hurdles
Maximise securityholder value and returns
Sustainable returns
above cost of equity
1. Based on statutory profit after tax excluding investment property revaluations in the Investments segment and other exceptional items, as determined by the Board. 2. Consolidated gearing calculated as net debt divided by total tangible assets less cash.
Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024

**Layout**

Single content slide with the title Capital allocation framework top-left at about 5.7% from the left and 10.6% from the top, and a section label Capital management with page number 20 in an outlined circle at top-right. A large rounded white card spans from x 5.7% to 94.3% and y 23.8% to 93.6%; inside it, a left intro paragraph sits in the upper-left, a central vertical flow diagram sits in the middle column, and a lower three-row table-like grid of navy and grey bars sits beneath, with a gold vertical rule and a right-hand summary at the right. Footnotes sit bottom-left and the footer credit bottom-right, both left and right aligned to the card margins.

**Visuals**

Diagram (vertical flow with brackets). Top: two equal light grey (#EBEBEB) rounded rectangles side by side, labelled Operating productivity and Capital productivity. A thin navy (#242444) horizontal line with a centred downward chevron, labelled Net operating cash flow above it. Below, two stacked solid navy (#242444) rounded bars labelled Strong balance sheet and 30-50% distribution payout ratio with superscript 1. A right-facing navy arc bracket spans the top two boxes and points to a dot-ended connector leading to the text Maximise cash flow through lower costs, productivity, technology and culture. A second arc bracket spans the two navy bars and connects via a dot-ended line to the text Distribution linked to business performance. Below that a second thin line with a downward chevron labelled Excess cash flow. Bottom: a three-row grid; each row has a navy rounded label bar on the left (Debt reduction, Capital return, Growth) and a light grey (#EBEBEB) rounded rectangle on the right (To target gearing level (5-15%) by the end of FY26 with superscript 2; Security buy-backs, additional distributions; Subject to strict investment hurdles). A vertical gold (#D4A03C) rule runs alongside the bottom grid, beside the summary heading. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid pale blue #DEF6FC full-bleed, with a white rounded card covering most of the slide. Pale sky-blue slide background (#DEF6FC) with a large white rounded card (corner radius about 2.5% of slide width, no visible shadow). Titles and primary text are dark navy #242444; grey light fills #EBEBEB; accent gold #D4A03C; footnote and footer text mid grey #7F7F7F. Typeface is CircularXX, a geometric humanist sans-serif, in Light weight for most text with Regular for emphasised words and the right-hand heading. Title is about 7.4% of slide height, intro text about 3.0%, body and diagram labels about 2.0%, header and footnotes about 1.3-1.7%. Lines are thin navy strokes about 1.5px; bars and boxes have small corner radii of about 0.6% of slide height; no gradients.

### Slide 24

**Review notes**

- Review: Many divider pages (about 15 of 47) are empty, slide 24 is unlabelled, and some slides lack a headline statement of what to take away, so the reader has to find the point.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent card layout, the figure-plus-label hierarchy of slide 12, the stepped flow of slide 23 with its brace annotations, and the segment colour coding that carries through slides 22 and 24.

Based on statutory profit after tax excluding investment property revaluations in the Investments segment and other exceptional items, as determined by the Board.
Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024

**Layout**

Single slide with the title top-left at about 5.7% from the left and 10.6% from the top, and a section label with circled page number 21 in the top-right corner. Below the title, one large rounded white card spans the slide from about 5.7% to 94.3% horizontally and 23.8% to 93.6% vertically; inside it two columns, left column Invested capital with two donut charts, right column Capital structure with three label-and-value rows, then a full-width divider with a downward chevron, then three dark pills in a row, and footnotes and footer text at the bottom outside the card.

**Visuals**

Two donut charts, no axes, no chart title beyond the section headings. Left donut titled Invested capital (superscript 1), legend Australia (dark navy square) and International (light blue square); dark navy segment labelled >75% at top, light blue segment labelled <25% at bottom (about a quarter of the ring, roughly 7 to 5 o'clock). Right donut with legend Investments (dark navy square) and Development (light blue square); dark navy segment labelled >60% at top, light blue segment labelled <40% at bottom (about 40% of the ring). Donut ring thickness about one third of the outer radius, small white gaps between segments, inner hole white. Right panel Capital structure is a three-row table: row 1 Consolidated gearing 2 with value 5-15% 3; row 2 Credit rating with value Investment grade; row 3 Distribution policy with value 30-50% payout ratio 4. Values sit in light blue rounded cells; rows separated by thin grey rules. Below the table a full-width navy divider line broken in the centre by a downward chevron icon. Three dark navy rounded rectangles with white text: Improved financial position; Higher quality, recurring income; Sustainable returns above cost of equity. No photographs, logos or icons other than the chevron.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #DEF6FC. Light sky-blue slide background #DEF6FC with a white rounded card #FFFFFF on top. Title and section headings and body text in dark navy #242444; accent fills dark navy #242444 and light blue #DEF6FC; footnotes and footer in grey #7F7F7F. Typeface CircularXX Light, a geometric sans-serif in light weight. Title height about 7.4% of slide height, section headings about 3.0%, body text about 2.2%, footnotes about 1.3%. Rules are thin navy lines about 2px. Pills and value cells have small corner radii, about 1.5% of slide width. No shadows or gradients.

None photographic. **Design**

Background light blue #DEF6FC. Inside, a white (#FFFFFF) panel with corner radius about 3% of slide width. ### Slide 32

**Text**

Structural options
29
Key structural options considered
Status quo
Internal restructuring and / accelerated capital release
Demerger
Description
• Simplifying the business through a / reallocation of capital to Australia / (40-60%) and Investments (50-70%)
• Internal separation of non-strategic / businesses and assets
• Separated business unit to focus / solely on maximising capital return
• Separate reporting with full / transparency on progress and KPIs
• Implementation of a structural / separation (e.g., Australia vs. / international)
• Delivers securityholders an / investment in two businesses with / different attributes and strategies
Key / considerations
• Currently delivering insufficient / returns on a risk-adjusted basis
• Limited securityholder appetite / for further investment into / underperforming markets and / segments
• Provides a clear pathway to / stabilisation and unlocking value / on a realistic timeline
• Balances trade-off between time / value of money and market timing
• Requires a capital injection
• Duplicates cost base
• Complex, costly and lengthy / implementation
Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024

**Layout**

A title sits top-left at about 5.7% from the left edge, above a large white rounded panel that spans roughly 5.7% to 94.3% horizontally and 23.8% to 93.6% vertically. Inside the panel, a table has a left label column and three option columns, with the middle column highlighted as a dark navy band; horizontal rules divide the column headers from the Description row and the Key considerations row. A section label and page-number circle sit top-right, and the footer is right-aligned at bottom right.

**Visuals**

None as images or charts. The slide is a table built from shapes and text: 4 columns by 3 rows. Columns: row-label column (no header), 'Status quo', 'Internal restructuring and accelerated capital release' (highlighted dark navy), 'Demerger'. Rows: 'Description' (bullets as listed in elements) and 'Key considerations' (bullets as listed in elements). Column header tiles are light grey #EBEBEB for Status quo and Demerger, and dark navy #242444 with white text for the middle column. The middle column's body cells are also dark navy with white text.

**Design**

Background light cyan #DEF6FC with a white rounded panel #FFFFFF over it. Titles and body text are navy #242444. White text is used inside the dark middle column. Accent is the navy #242444 used for the highlighted column and the header rule. Footer and small furniture are grey #7F7F7F except the section label and page number, which are navy #242444. Typeface is CircularXX, a geometric sans-serif, in Light for the title and bullet text and Regular for the column headers and row labels. Title is about 7.4% of slide height; body text about 2.2%; footer about 1.3%. Shapes have rounded corners (panel about 3.6% of slide height, column tiles about 1.6%, dark column about 2.2%), no shadows, no gradients. Rules are thin, about 1 px, navy under the headers and grey at the lower two dividers.

**Layout**

Full-bleed light blue slide with a large white rounded panel spanning most of the width below the title block. Title 'Investments' sits top-left at about x 5.7%, with the subtitle beneath it; 'Segment summaries' and a circled page number 31 sit top-right. Inside the panel, the left column holds three statements with gold rules, a grey KPI card and a donut chart; the right column holds a bar chart with legend above and four grey KPI tiles below. Footnotes are bottom-left and the footer 'Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024' is bottom-right, both small grey text on the blue background.

**Visuals**

Left KPI card: grey rounded tile reading '>70' over 'capital partners', a short navy divider line, then '10%' over 'FUM CAGR, FY19-23 1,2'. Donut chart: ring with centre label '$50.4b' over 'FUM, HY24 1,2'. Segments clockwise from top: Australia 62% (navy #242444, largest, right and bottom), Asia 24% (pale blue #DEF6FC, left), Americas 5% (gold #D7A04B, upper-left), Europe 9% (light grey #EBEBEB, top). Legend below the donut: navy square Australia, pale blue square Asia, gold square Americas, light grey square Europe. Bar chart titled 'Historical performance (incl. international) 1' with legend ROIC (navy square) and Average (gold square). Vertical navy bars with value labels above, no printed y-axis or range: FY19 7.3%, FY20 8.6%, FY21 5.5%, FY22 7.5%, FY23 5.5%; category labels FY19 to FY23 below a navy baseline. A horizontal gold line at about 6.9% spans FY19 to beyond FY23, labelled 6.9% in gold at its right end. Four grey KPI tiles below the bar chart: $123m base management revenue, HY24 1,3; 41.2% avg. EBITDA margin, FY19-23 1,3,4; 6.2% co-investment % of FUM, HY24 1; 3.1% co-investment yield, HY24 1,5. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background pale blue #DEF6FC with a white rounded panel #FFFFFF. Titles and body text navy #242444. Accents gold #D7A04B (short rules, Americas segment, average line, 6.9% label). Footnotes and footer grey #7F7F7F. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to Circular, light weight for the title, subtitle and statements, regular for the chart heading and 6.9% label, bold for the gold dashes. Title about 7.4% of slide height, subtitle about 4.1%, statements about 3.0%, chart labels and body about 1.7% to 1.9%, footnotes about 1.3%. Grey KPI tiles #EBEBEB with rounded corners of roughly 2% of slide width; no shadows or gradients. Thin navy baseline under the bars. The white panel has rounded corners of about 3% of slide width.

Segment summaries

Focused on improving profitability in Europe and the Americas

**Layout**

Two-column content slide on a light blue field: a large white rounded card spans nearly the full width below the subtitle, with a lead statement and bullet list in the left column (about 8% to 30% of width) and a financial table in the right column (about 34% to 92%). The title sits top-left at about 10.6% from the top, left-aligned to a 5.7% margin, with the subtitle beneath it; the section label 'Segment summaries' and page number 32 sit top-right; the footnotes run along the bottom left and the report name bottom right, both at about 96% of the height.

**Layout**

Full-bleed pale cyan slide with a title block at top left: large title "Development" at about 5.7% from left and 10.6% from top, with a subtitle line beneath it at 19.4%. A small header at top right reads "Segment summaries" followed by a circled page number 33. A single large white rounded card spans from x 5.7% to 94.3% and y 28.9% to 93.6%, split into two columns: left column (x 8.4% to about 47%) holds three statements with gold rules and two donut charts with legends; right column (x 50.8% to 91.5%) holds a bar chart with its title and legend, and four grey KPI cards across the bottom of the card. Footnotes sit bottom left at about y 95.3% and the footer text "Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024" sits bottom right at x 81.8%, y 96.1%. Margins are about 5.7% left and 5.7% right.

**Visuals**

Two donut charts, one bar chart with an average line, and four KPI cards.
Donut 1 (left): title none; centre label "$10.4b work in progress HY24 2,4". Segments clockwise from 12 o'clock: Residential (BTS) 65% navy #242444 (large segment, lower right and bottom); Residential (BTR) 5% pale cyan #DEF6FC; Workplace 24% gold #D7A04B (left side); Other 6% light grey #EBEBEB (top, just left of 12 o'clock). Labels: 65% in white at lower right of ring, 5% navy, 24% navy, 6% navy. Thin white separators between segments. Legend below: Residential (BTS) 5 navy swatch, Residential (BTR) 5 pale cyan swatch, Workplace gold swatch, Other light grey swatch.
Donut 2 (centre): centre label "$10.4b work in progress HY24 2,4". Segments clockwise from 12 o'clock: Joint venture 78% navy #242444 (large segment); Fund-through 16% pale cyan #DEF6FC (upper left); Balance sheet 6% gold #D7A04B (top, just right of 12 o'clock). Labels: 78% in white at lower right, 16% navy at upper left, 6% navy at top. Legend below: Joint venture navy swatch, Fund-through pale cyan swatch, Balance sheet gold swatch.
Bar chart: vertical column chart titled "Historical performance (Australia) 2,3". Legend top right: ROIC navy square and Average gold square. Y axis not shown and no units printed; values printed above bars. Categories on x axis: FY19, FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23 (no axis title). Series ROIC, navy #242444 bars, printed values: FY19 31.1%, FY20 11.4%, FY21 28.6%, FY22 12.0%, FY23 7.8%. Bars are drawn to scale against a baseline at about y 67.7%: FY19 tallest, FY21 second tallest. Average series is a single horizontal gold #D7A04B line at 18.2% (about y 53.5%) running from about x 54.0% to 86.4%, with label "18.2%" in gold at its right end. Baseline is a navy horizontal line under the bars from x 50.0% to 90.4%. No highlighted points beyond the average line.
KPI cards: four rounded light grey #EBEBEB cards with big navy numbers: "$10.4b" over "work in progress HY24 2,4"; "$2.9b" over "master planned or in conversion, HY24 2"; "$1.8b" over "invested capital HY24 2"; "18.2%" over "avg. ROIC FY19-23 2".

**Design**

Background pale cyan #DEF6FC. Main white card #FFFFFF with rounded corners of about 2% of slide width, no visible border or shadow. Titles and headline statements navy #242444. Body and chart labels navy #242444. Footnotes and footer grey #7F7F7F. Accents gold #D7A04B (average line, rules, Workplace and Balance sheet segments, 18.2% label). Data segment colours: navy #242444, pale cyan #DEF6FC, gold #D7A04B, light grey #EBEBEB. Typeface: Circular-style geometric sans-serif (CircularXX), Light weight for most text, Regular for the chart title and the 18.2% label, Bold for gold rule dashes. Title text about 7.4% of slide height, subtitle about 4.1%, statements about 3.0%, chart and body labels about 1.7%, footnotes about 1.3%. Shapes: KPI cards light grey #EBEBEB with corner radius about 2% of slide width; donut rings flat with white separators; bars flat, square-cornered.
