**Layout**

Full-bleed light blue slide with a left margin of about 5.7% and a right margin of about 5.7%. The title Development sits top-left at about 10.6% from the top, with the subtitle Summary of select Australian projects directly beneath it; a small header label Segment summaries and a circled page number 34 sit top-right at about 6% from the top. A large white rounded card occupies the middle and lower body (y 28.9% to 93.6%) and holds a nine-column table with a two-tier header; footnotes sit bottom-left and the footer Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024 sits bottom-right, both on the same baseline band at about 95% to 97%.

**Visuals**

One table, no chart, photograph, logo or icon. Table columns (left to right): Project; City; Sector (superscript 2); Model (superscript 3); Ownership; Invested capital (superscript 4) spanning FY23 and HY24; End value (superscript 5) spanning $b; Margin (superscript 6) spanning %; Delivery timeline spanning Start and Target completion. Rows: a grey group row Development (Australia only) (superscript 1) with no values; then One Sydney Harbour R1/R2, Sydney, Residential (BTS), Joint venture, 75%, FY23 0.4, HY24 0.6, $b 3.7, margin 30-40%, Start FY20, Target completion FY25; One Sydney Harbour R3, Sydney, Residential (BTS), Balance sheet, 100%, 0.1, 0.2, 0.6, 0-10%, FY22, FY25; One Circular Quay, Sydney, Residential (BTS), Hotel, Joint venture, 33%, 0.1, 0.2, 3.1, 30-40%, FY23, FY27; Victoria Harbour, Melbourne, Residential (BTS and BTR), Balance sheet, 100%, 0.1, 0.1, 2.3, 10-20%, FY24, FY30; Melbourne Quarter West, Melbourne, Residential (BTR), Joint venture, 25%, 0.0, 0.0, 0.5, 10-20%, FY23, FY26; Melbourne Quarter Tower, Melbourne, Workplace, Fund-through, 0%, 0.0, 0.0, 1.2, 10-20%, FY21, FY24; Victoria Cross OSD (superscript 7), Sydney, Workplace, Joint venture, 75%, 0.2, 0.3, 1.2, 10-20%, FY23, FY25; Town Hall Place, Melbourne, Workplace, Balance sheet, 100%, 0.0, 0.0, 0.4, n.a. (superscript 8), FY25, FY27. No values are printed as charts; no highlighted cells.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #DEF6FC, full slide. Light sky-blue background with a flat white card, no gradient or texture. Titles and body text are dark navy #242444; footnotes and footer are mid grey #7F7F7F; the only accent is the navy rules and the grey group band. Typeface is CircularXX Light, a geometric sans-serif in light weight; title about 7.4% of slide height (capital height about 5.2%), subtitle about 4.1%, column headers and body about 1.85%, footnotes and footer about 1.3%. The card has a generous corner radius of about 3% of slide height and no border or shadow. Rules: a 2px navy line under the Invested capital, End value, Margin and Delivery timeline group labels and a 2px navy rule under the column header row; thin 1px light grey horizontal row dividers; a 1px grey bottom rule closing the table. Circle around page number is a thin 1px navy outline.

### Slide 38

**Text**

Segment summaries / 35 / Construction / Australia-only builder with deep sector expertise and client relationships / Delivery capability for integrated model / Exposure to a diverse range of sectors / Heritage of operational excellence / Historical performance (Australia) 1 / EBITDA margin / Average / 3.9% / 3.8% / 3.1% / 3.0% / 2.8% / 3.3% / FY19 / FY20 / FY21 / FY22 / FY23 / 7% / 3% / 8% / 19% / 81% / 23% / 59% / $4.5b / backlog / revenue / HY24 1 / Government / Corporate / Social infrastructure / Defence / Workplace / Residential / Other / $4.5b / backlog / revenue HY24 1 / $4.5b / backlog revenue, HY24 1 / $6.5b / external preferred, HY24 1 / $3.4b / avg. revenue FY19-23 1 / 3.3% / avg. EBITDA margin, FY19-23 1 / 1. Australia only. Excludes Engineering and Services. / Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024

**Layout**

Full-bleed pale aqua slide with a left-aligned title and subtitle stacked at the top-left margin (about 5.7% from the left edge), and a page marker with a circled number at top right. A single large white rounded card spans about 5.7% to 94.3% of the width and 28.9% to 93.6% of the height; inside it, three left-hand statements sit above two donut charts in two columns, with a bar chart and four grey KPI tiles on the right; the footnote sits bottom left and the date bottom right, both at about 96% height.

**Visuals**

Two donut charts and one bar chart with a horizontal average line, plus four KPI tiles. Donut 1 titled by centre text '$4.5b backlog revenue HY24 1', no axes, segments: Government 81% navy #242444 (about 81%, printed value) and Corporate 19% pale aqua #DEF6FC (printed 19%); legend below as navy square Government and pale aqua square Corporate. Donut 2 with centre text '$4.5b backlog revenue HY24 1', segments printed: Social infrastructure 59% navy #242444, Defence 23% pale aqua #DEF6FC, Workplace 8% gold #D7A04B, Residential 3% light grey #EBEBEB, Other 7% mid grey #8C8C8C; legend two rows: Social infrastructure, Defence / Workplace, Residential, Other. Donut rings are about 26% of the diameter as hole size, with 1 to 2 degree white gaps between segments. Bar chart titled 'Historical performance (Australia) 1', legend top right: navy square 'EBITDA margin' and gold square 'Average'. Bars navy #242444, no y-axis or gridlines, baseline navy line; values printed above bars: FY19 3.1%, FY20 3.0%, FY21 3.9%, FY22 3.8%, FY23 2.8%; category labels FY19 to FY23 below baseline. A horizontal gold #D7A04B line at about 3.3% runs across the bars from about FY19 to FY23 at about 0.3% of slide height thick, labelled 3.3% in gold at its right end. KPI tiles: '$4.5b' over 'backlog revenue, HY24 1', '$6.5b' over 'external preferred, HY24 1', '$3.4b' over 'avg. revenue FY19-23 1', '3.3%' over 'avg. EBITDA margin, FY19-23 1'. Left statements are separated by three short gold rules. No photographs or logos.

**Design**

Background pale aqua #DEF6FC with a white rounded card #FFFFFF; titles and body text navy #242444; accents gold #D7A04B for rules, average line and one donut segment; secondary grey #7F7F7F for footer; KPI tiles light grey #EBEBEB. Typeface is CircularXX, a geometric humanist sans-serif, Light weight for title, subtitle, statements, labels and values, Regular for the chart heading and the superscript 1 in gold. Title about 7.4% of slide height, subtitle about 4.1%, statements about 3.0%, chart headings about 2.2%, body labels about 1.7%, footer about 1.3%. Rules are thin gold lines about 0.2% high; cards have soft rounded corners of about 2.5% of slide width, with no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Title block sits top-left with the title at x5.7% and the subtitle directly beneath it; a section label and a circled page number sit top-right. A large white rounded panel spans x5.7 to 94.3% and y28.9 to 93.6%, holding a narrow left text column (about x8.4 to 28.8%) and two equal right columns (x34.7 to 61.9% and x64.1 to 91.4%), each with a navy header box above a grey rounded list box. Footnotes sit bottom-left and a footer line sits bottom-right, both inside the light-blue margin.

**Visuals**

None photographic or charted. Non-text elements are flat geometric shapes: a full-slide light-blue background rectangle, a large white rounded panel, two navy rounded header bars (#242444), two light-grey rounded list panels (#EBEBEB) and a thin navy circle outline around the page number 36. No logos, icons, images or charts. The two grey panels hold centred text lists, and the right-hand panel has a grey italic note at its foot.

**Design**

Background: Solid light blue #DEF6FC (a white rectangle lies beneath it, fully covered). Light-blue background #DEF6FC with a white rounded content panel (#FFFFFF) and no shadows or gradients. Title and body text are navy #242444; accents are gold #D7A04B for the two column headings; footnotes and footer are grey #7F7F7F; the italic note is light grey #A6A6A6. Typeface is CircularXX, a geometric humanist sans-serif in Light weight. Title is about 7.4% of slide height, subtitle about 4.1%, body and list text about 2.2 to 3.0%, footnotes about 1.3%. Panels and header bars have rounded corners of roughly 3 to 4% of slide height with no outlines.

**Layout**

Full-bleed light blue background with a large white rounded card spanning most of the slide below the title. The title sits top-left at about 10.6% from the top, with a section header 'Business simplification' and a circled page number '39' at top right. Left column holds the two-paragraph statement (about 8% to 23% of width); the right area holds the chart title with a rule underneath and a waterfall chart beneath, with the x-axis labels at the bottom of the card. Footnote bottom-left and footer 'Lendlease Strategy Update - May 2024' bottom-right, both at about 96% from the top.

**Visuals**

Waterfall (bridge) chart titled 'FTE reductions from productivity initiatives and divestments', unit FTEs, no printed y-axis or gridlines; baseline is a dark navy horizontal axis line at about 85% of slide height spanning the chart width. Bars left to right with printed values: FY21 total bar light grey #EBEBEB labelled 9,900 (superscript 1) and FTEs, height about 35% of slide; a stacked bar labelled 2,250 with a grey segment labelled 1,850 divestments on top and a thinner grey segment labelled 400 productivity initiatives below (segment separated by a thin line); FY23 bar light grey #EBEBEB labelled 7,650 FTEs; a small grey floating step labelled 690 productivity initiatives; HY24 navy #242444 bar labelled 6,960 FTEs; a thin gold #D7A04B step labelled 100 productivity initiatives (FY24); a thin gold step labelled 250 - 350 organisational restructuring (FY25); a gold #D7A04B block labelled 2,150 divestments; final navy #242444 bar labelled 4,400 FTEs, ending at the 'within 18 months' label. Highlight: a navy callout box '>35% reduction within 18 months' with a navy connector line from its base down to a diagonal navy line running from the HY24 bar top (dot marker) to the 4,400 bar top (dot marker), i.e. a bracket spanning HY24 to within 18 months. Values are printed, not estimated. No legend, no photographs, no logos.

**Design**

Background light cyan #DEF6FC. Card white #FFFFFF with rounded corners of about 2.5% of slide height radius and no visible shadow. Titles and main text navy #242444; chart value labels dark grey #3A3A39; footnote and footer grey #7F7F7F; accents gold #D7A04B; neutral bars #EBEBEB. Typeface CircularXX Light, a geometric sans-serif in light weight. Title size about 7.4% of slide height; body and chart title about 3.0%; chart value labels about 1.85%; category labels about 1.1% to 1.7%; footer about 1.3%. Lines: navy rule under chart title about 0.2% thick; navy axis line about 0.3% thick; callout connector navy about 0.3% thick.

**Design**

Background: Pale aqua #DEF6FC outer field with a white #FFFFFF rounded panel covering nearly the whole slide. Light panel-on-tint style with a pale aqua outer field and a large white rounded panel. Titles in a light geometric sans-serif (Circular-like) in dark navy #242444, body and table figures in the same light sans in navy #242444 with some figures #323232, footnotes and source in grey #7F7F7F. Title is about 4.1 percent of slide height, table text about 1.5 percent, footnotes about 1.3 percent. Section band fills #EBEBEB with navy text. Thin navy rules #242444 about 0.2 percent of height separate header and bands, with light grey hairline row dividers. Page number sits in a thin navy outlined circle with no fill. No shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Full-slide light cyan (#DEF6FC) margin surrounds a large white rounded card inset about 1.5% from the left and right edges and 2.6% from top and bottom. The title sits top-left at x 5.7%, y 11.1%, with a section label 'Financials' and page number '42' in a small circle at top right (x 86.6% to 93.7%, y 6%). Two identical four-block tables sit side by side in two columns: left table x 5.7% to 41.8% and right table x 52.7% to 94.4%, both starting at y 20.3% and ending near y 86%, with a footnote block at bottom left (x 5.7%, y 92.6%) and the source line bottom right (x 81.8%, y 94.1%), both left or right aligned to the card margins.

**Visuals**

Two financial tables, no charts or images. Left table: columns $m, FY19, FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, 5yr avg., HY24. Four blocks: Corporate costs (rows I / D / C (ex-CRU), Capital Release Unit, Group); Net debt (closing) 2 (same three rows); Investment property revaluations 3,4 (same three rows); Other exceptional items 3,5 (same three rows). Values are printed in parentheses for negatives, dashes for nil. Right table: columns $m, FY19, FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, 5yr avg., HY24. Four blocks: Profit after tax 6,7; Earnings per security (cents) 6,7; Return on Equity 6,7; Return on Equity (incl. investment property revaluations) 4,7; each with the same three rows. Shaded grey header bands (#EBEBEB) sit behind each block title, and thin grey horizontal rules separate rows. No highlighted cells.

**Design**

Background colour cyan #DEF6FC frame with white #FFFFFF card. Titles and table text dark navy #242444; footnotes and source line mid grey #7F7F7F; negative values in the HY24 column use #323232. Accent: grey band #EBEBEB. Typeface CircularXX Light, a geometric sans-serif, light weight. Slide title about 4.1% of slide height (approx 5.7 percent cap height); table text about 1.5% of slide height; footnotes about 1.3%; section label and page number about 1.7%. Card corner radius large, roughly 5% of slide height. Thin hairline grey rules #A0A0A0 at about 0.1% of slide height separate table rows; the table header line is a darker navy rule about 0.3% thick. No shadows or gradients.
